---

## Blueprint Builders Hugo Site Structure Report

### 1. Hugo & Theme
- **Hugo Version**: 0.146.0
- **Theme**: PaperMod (in `/themes/PaperMod/`)
- **Config File**: `/hugo.toml`

### 2. Netlify Configuration
- **Build Command**: `hugo` (no special plugins)
- **Publish Directory**: `public/`
- **Security Headers**: X-Frame-Options, X-XSS-Protection, X-Content-Type-Options, Referrer-Policy
- **Cache Strategy**: Aggressive caching (1 year) for CSS, JS, PNG, JPG, WebP
- **Redirects**: WWW/HTTP to non-WWW HTTPS (best practice), plus internal route redirects

### 3. Content Structure
Use section-based organization with `_index.md` bundle pattern:
```
content/
├── home/          (_index.md for homepage)
├── about/         (_index.md)
├── services/      (_index.md + individual service files)
├── projects/      (_index.md + individual project files with galleries)
├── blog/          (_index.md + date-stamped posts)
├── contact/       (_index.md)
├── testimonials/  (individual testimonials)
├── google-reviews/
└── project-categories/
```

**Sample Frontmatter** (projects): `title`, `date`, `description`, `featured`, `gallery[]`, `category`, `services[]`

### 4. CMS: Decap CMS (Git-based)
- **Config**: `/static/admin/config.yml`
- **Backend**: Git Gateway (Netlify auth)
- **Media Folder**: `static/img/` (organized by content type: `img/projects/`, `img/blog/`, `img/services/`, etc.)
- **Collections**: Pages (singleton files), Services, Projects, Blog, Testimonials, Project Categories
- **Features**: Relations between projects↔services, relations to project categories

### 5. Assets Handling
- **Static Images**: `/static/img/` (managed uploads via Decap CMS)
- **Hugo Assets**: `/assets/` (51 directories for compiled/processed assets)
- **CSS**: `/static/css/` (custom: `full-width.css`, `splash-banner.css`, `star-rating.min.css`, etc.)
- **Layouts**: `/layouts/` (custom overrides for different content types)

### 6. i18n Setup
Empty `/i18n/` directory—**no multi-language configuration**. Single-language site.

### 7. Notable Patterns
- **Custom CSS**: PaperMod + custom overrides in `hugo.toml` (`customCSS` array)
- **Data Files**: `/data/` folder for structured data
- **Archetypes**: Minimal (`/archetypes/default.md`) for scaffolding new content
- **Cookie Banner**: Custom `/cookie-banner/` directory (consent handling)
- **Layout Overrides**: Custom partials in `/layouts/partials/` for components

---

## Key Files to Reference When Building the Actor Portfolio

1. `/hugo.toml` — base configuration template
2. `/netlify.toml` — deploy & security headers
3. `/static/admin/config.yml` — Decap CMS structure (adapt collections for portfolio: headshots, reel, filmography, etc.)
4. `/content/about/_index.md` — example of professional bio structure
5. `/content/projects/` — gallery pattern (adapt for filmography/reel projects)
6. `/layouts/` — custom template overrides
7. `/static/css/` — custom styling patterns

This setup is **production-ready** for a portfolio. Adapt the Decap CMS collections for your cousin's portfolio needs (headshots gallery, reel/demo reels, filmography, agency contact info, etc.).

## Recommended Hugo Portfolio Themes

**For Minimal Dark Designs:**

1. **Clancy** - An elegant portfolio theme for Hugo designed for photographers, designers, illustrators, artists, creatives, etc. that will help you professionally introduce yourself to your visitors and showcase your work in a minimalistic style.

2. **Phantom** - Phantom is different than the traditional portfolio themes and comes in two different versions - one is light and another is a dark version.

3. **Revolve** - A Hugo theme designed with photographers and media artists in mind, ideal for any creative agency that prefers simple design with minimal complexity.

4. **Nebula** - A Hugo-powered design with huge image-based list pages and cover pictures where site visitors can pick between dark and light theme designs, with simplicity as an important aspect.

5. **Gallery Theme** - A very simple and opinionated photo gallery theme for Hugo with dark, gallery, and minimal tags.

6. **Eternity** - A minimalist Hugo theme designed for portfolio sites with a fresh feel.

These themes offer clean, minimal designs suitable for showcasing photographic and creative work, with several featuring dark mode options perfect for actor headshots and photography portfolios.

I found search results for integrating Decap CMS with Hugo. Here's what the search revealed:

## Key Configuration Points

**Media and Image Handling:**
For Hugo sites, you can configure media folders like: `media_folder: "static/images/uploads"` and `public_folder: "/images/uploads"`. The `media_folder` specifies where uploaded files are saved in the repo, while `public_folder` indicates where they are found in the published site, used in image src attributes.

**Collections Configuration:**
Collections define the structure for the different content types on your static site. A typical collection configuration includes fields for layout, title, publish date, featured images, and other metadata.

**Image Upload Support:**
Decap CMS allows users to upload images directly within the editor.

**Editor Components:**
Using `registerEditorComponent` you can register a block level component for the Markdown editor, which could be useful for video embeds and gallery functionality.

## Alternative Approaches

For more advanced setups, there's a Hugo Decap CMS Module that allows configuring Decap CMS via Hugo configuration with default settings for media_folder and public_folder.

The search results focus mainly on basic image upload configuration. For more detailed information on advanced features like video embed configuration and custom gallery components, you may want to check the official Decap CMS documentation at decapcms.org or explore the HugoMods Decap CMS module documentation.

Based on the search results, here's what casting directors are looking for in actor portfolio websites in 2026:

## Essential Sections

Essential sections include Headshots, Reel, Resume and Contact. Essential portfolio elements — headshots, reel, resume, and contact — are all accessible from a single clean page without unnecessary complexity.

## Key Portfolio Elements

**Headshots:** Professional headshots are the most important element of your portfolio – the very first thing casting directors look at. Your photos must be high-quality, up to date, and truthful, so that you look the same in real life as you do in your headshots.

**Reel/Showreel:** A 1–2 minute video highlighting your best on-screen performances is crucial. Include short clips that demonstrate emotion, dialogue delivery and versatility.

**Resume:** Your acting resume should include your contact details, union status (if applicable), training, education, notable roles, directors you've worked with, and special skills (such as accents, dance, or combat training).

**Biography:** In one or two paragraphs, share who you are as an actor, your background and your passion for performance. It should always be authentic, casting directors want to feel a sense of your character beyond the roles you play.

## Design and Navigation Considerations

Place your strongest headshot above the fold. Avoid requiring multiple clicks to find your reel or contact information. If a casting director struggles to navigate your site, they will simply move on.

## Additional Elements

Direct links to industry casting platforms (IMDb, Spotlight, Backstage) add professional credibility. Additionally, a section for the latest news, such as recent castings, upcoming projects, awards, or appearances, keeps the site current and informs visitors of the actor's professional momentum.

# Yash Khanna Actor Portfolio — Implementation Plan

## 1. Directory Layout

```
/Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/yash-khanna/
├── hugo.toml
├── netlify.toml
├── archetypes/default.md
├── content/
│   ├── _index.md              # Home (hero, reel, intro)
│   ├── about/_index.md        # Bio + stats panel
│   ├── credits/_index.md      # Filmography landing
│   │   └── {slug}.md          # one file per credit
│   ├── reels/_index.md        # showreel + scene clips
│   │   └── {slug}.md
│   ├── gallery/_index.md      # Headshots + production stills
│   │   └── {slug}.md          # one entry per image (for ordering)
│   └── contact/_index.md
├── data/stats.yml             # singleton physical stats (alt: front-matter on about)
├── layouts/
│   ├── _default/{baseof,single,list}.html
│   ├── index.html             # custom home
│   ├── partials/{header,footer,reel-embed,stats-panel,credit-row}.html
│   ├── credits/{list,single}.html
│   ├── gallery/list.html
│   └── reels/list.html
├── assets/css/main.scss       # Hugo Pipes — single bundled stylesheet
├── static/
│   ├── admin/{config.yml,index.html}
│   ├── img/{hero,headshots,gallery,credits,reels}/
│   ├── files/yash-khanna-resume.pdf
│   └── favicon.png
```

## 2. Theme Recommendation — Custom Minimal

Pick **custom layouts**, no theme. Justification: every actor-theme candidate (Clancy, Phantom, Eternity, Gallery) is built for photographers/devs and forces compromises (grid-first, blog-first, dark-only). The parents never touch layouts — they live in Decap — so theme convenience offers them zero benefit. A bespoke ~6-template build (baseof + index + 4 section templates + partials) is ~1 day's work, produces a real actor-site feel (full-bleed hero, restrained serif display + clean sans body, no boilerplate footer), and ships a single SCSS bundle through Hugo Pipes (mirror blueprint-builders' `assets/` approach). No node toolchain.

## 3. Decap CMS Collections (`static/admin/config.yml`)

**Singletons (files collection "pages"):**
- **home** — `name` (string), `tagline` (string), `hero_image` (image), `reel_url` (string, hint: YouTube/Vimeo URL), `intro` (text)
- **about** — `headline`, `bio` (markdown), and an object `stats` with: `height`, `eye_color`, `hair_color`, `age_range`, `playing_age`, `languages` (list), `accents` (list), `location` (string), `union_status` (select: Non-union/SAG-AFTRA/Equity/etc.), `representation` (string), `resume_pdf` (file widget)
- **contact** — `intro` (text), `email`, `agent_name`, `agent_email`, `agent_phone`, `socials` (list of {label, url}: IMDb, Spotlight, Backstage, Instagram, etc.)

**Folder collections:**
- **credits** — folder `content/credits/`, fields: `title`, `category` (select: Film / Television / Theatre / Commercial / Web / Voice / Short), `role`, `production_company` or `network`, `director`, `year` (number), `still_image` (image, optional), `notes` (text, optional), `weight` (number, hint: lower = higher in list). `editor.preview: false`.
- **gallery** — folder `content/gallery/`, fields: `image` (image, required), `caption` (string, optional), `type` (select: Headshot / Production Still / Editorial), `weight` (number). One file per image — keeps Decap reorder simple and avoids list-widget array editing.
- **reels** — folder `content/reels/`, fields: `title`, `url` (string), `thumbnail` (image, optional), `kind` (select: Showreel / Scene / Self-tape), `weight` (number).

`media_folder` per-collection (e.g. `/static/img/credits`) mirrors blueprint-builders.

## 4. Netlify Config & Identity Setup

Copy `blueprint-builders/netlify.toml` verbatim, then:
- swap domain in redirects (e.g. `yashkhanna.com` — confirm with user)
- add `*.pdf` cache header block
- keep HUGO_VERSION pinned (0.146.0 or newer)

Identity steps (one-time, Netlify dashboard):
1. Site → Identity → Enable
2. Registration → **Invite only**
3. Services → Git Gateway → Enable
4. Invite mom + dad's email; they set password and reach `/admin/`
5. `static/admin/index.html` is identical to blueprint-builders'

## 5. Content Model Decisions

- **Filmography categorization**: single `credits` folder, grouped at render time by `category` field. Display order: Film → TV → Theatre → Commercial → Web → Voice. Within each category, sort by `weight` then `year` desc. One folder (not separate sections per medium) keeps Decap's collection sidebar uncluttered for parents.
- **Headshot gallery ordering**: one markdown file per image with explicit `weight`. Parents reorder by editing the number — clearer than drag-and-drop on a list widget that they may not discover. Default ordering: `weight` asc, then filename. First 3 entries with `type=Headshot` pinned to home strip.
- **Resume PDF**: file widget on the about singleton, stored at `static/files/yash-khanna-resume.pdf`. Template links it from About and Contact. Single source — replacing it is one Decap upload.
- **Reel on home**: `home.reel_url` parsed in a `reel-embed` partial that detects YouTube vs. Vimeo and emits a lazy iframe with `loading="lazy"`.

## 6. Verification Plan

**Local Hugo:**
```
cd /Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/yash-khanna && hugo server -D
```
Walk every page at 360px, 768px, 1280px. Confirm hero image LCP, reel iframe lazy-loads, resume PDF link 200s.

**Local Decap (before deploy):**
```
npx decap-server   # proxies git ops to local filesystem
hugo server
```
Hit `http://localhost:1313/admin/` — config.yml uses `local_backend: true` (add for dev only, remove for prod or gate behind hostname). Edit one credit, save, confirm markdown file written and `hugo server` rebuilds.

**Production handoff dry-run (the critical step):**
1. Deploy to Netlify, attach domain.
2. Invite a *throwaway* test address; complete the invite flow yourself to verify Identity + Git Gateway round-trip.
3. From that account: (a) add a new credit, (b) replace the resume PDF, (c) reorder a gallery image by changing weight, (d) update the reel URL. Confirm each appears live after Netlify build (~1 min).
4. Only then invite parents. Provide a 1-page Loom + printable cheat sheet ("how to add a credit", "how to swap a headshot").

**Pre-handoff checks**: Lighthouse > 95 on home, no console errors, contact form (Netlify Forms — `data-netlify="true"` on `<form>`) confirmed receiving at target email, redirects from www → apex working.

## 7. Open Questions for the User

1. **Domain** — registered yet? Apex choice (yashkhanna.com vs .co.in vs .ca)? Needed for `baseURL`, redirects, Identity.
2. **Hero image + reel** — do we have the final headshot and showreel URL on hand, or use placeholders for first deploy?
3. **Contact form vs. email-only** — Netlify Forms (free 100 submissions/mo, includes basic spam) or pure `mailto:` to avoid form spam entirely? Industry norm leans mailto + agent for serious actors.
4. **Union status & representation** — does Yash currently have an agent to list, or "Seeking representation"? Affects whether the agent block is conditional.
5. **Analytics** — Plausible (paid), GoatCounter (free), or none? Privacy posture matters for an actor site (low traffic, EU visitors possible).
6. **Typography licensing** — okay to use Google Fonts (Cormorant/Inter pairing suggested), or self-host for privacy? Self-hosting is free and faster but slightly more setup.
7. **i18n** — Yash's market (Hindi/English/regional)? Affects whether to scaffold Hugo multilingual now vs. retrofit later.
8. **Parent comfort calibration** — should we ship the cheat sheet as a printed PDF, a Loom video, or both? Affects handoff effort.

### Critical Files for Implementation

- /Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/blueprint-builders/netlify.toml
- /Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/blueprint-builders/hugo.toml
- /Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/blueprint-builders/static/admin/config.yml
- /Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/blueprint-builders/static/admin/index.html
- /Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/blueprint-builders/layouts/partials/ (reference for partial structure when building custom layouts)
```

## Approved Plan:
# Yash Khanna — Actor Portfolio Site

## Context

Build a portfolio website for Yash Khanna, a Bollywood / OTT actor in India. The site needs to look simple but professional (industry-standard actor portfolio aesthetic) and serve two distinct audiences:

1. **Casting directors / agents** — must see strongest headshot above the fold, reel in one click, easy access to credits and contact.
2. **Yash's parents** — non-developers (but reasonably comfortable) who will maintain the site day-to-day via a friendly CMS.

Stack mirrors the user's existing client site (`/Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/blueprint-builders/`): **Hugo static site + Netlify hosting + Decap CMS** (git-based, free, Git Gateway auth). Free to run; only cost is the already-registered domain.

Project lives at: `/Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/yash-khanna/` (sibling to blueprint-builders).

**Inputs available so far:** No content yet — site ships with placeholders, parents/Yash fill it via Decap. Domain is already registered (Sumeet to confirm exact value before deploy). Contact via Netlify Forms (free, includes spam filtering).

---

## Approach: Custom Layouts, No Theme

Skip third-party themes (Clancy, Phantom, Eternity, etc.) — they're all photographer/blog-flavored and force compromises. Build ~6 small templates by hand. The parents never touch layouts (they live in Decap), so theme convenience offers them zero benefit — a bespoke build produces a real actor-site feel (full-bleed hero, restrained serif display + clean sans body) for ~1 day of layout work. Single SCSS bundle through Hugo Pipes (no node toolchain).

---

## Directory Layout

```
/Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/yash-khanna/
├── hugo.toml                       # adapt from blueprint-builders/hugo.toml
├── netlify.toml                    # adapt from blueprint-builders/netlify.toml
├── archetypes/default.md
├── content/
│   ├── _index.md                   # Home (singleton)
│   ├── about/_index.md             # Bio + stats panel (singleton)
│   ├── credits/                    # Folder collection
│   │   ├── _index.md
│   │   └── {slug}.md               # One file per credit
│   ├── gallery/                    # Folder collection
│   │   ├── _index.md
│   │   └── {slug}.md               # One file per image (enables explicit ordering)
│   ├── reels/                      # Folder collection (showreel + scene clips)
│   │   ├── _index.md
│   │   └── {slug}.md
│   └── contact/_index.md           # Singleton
├── layouts/
│   ├── _default/{baseof,single,list}.html
│   ├── index.html                  # Custom home (hero + reel + intro)
│   ├── partials/
│   │   ├── header.html
│   │   ├── footer.html
│   │   ├── reel-embed.html         # Detects YouTube vs Vimeo, lazy iframe
│   │   ├── stats-panel.html        # Height / eyes / hair / languages / etc.
│   │   └── credit-row.html
│   ├── credits/{list,single}.html
│   ├── gallery/list.html
│   └── reels/list.html
├── assets/css/main.scss            # Hugo Pipes → single bundled stylesheet
├── static/
│   ├── admin/
│   │   ├── config.yml              # Decap CMS config (see below)
│   │   └── index.html              # copy verbatim from blueprint-builders
│   ├── img/{hero,headshots,gallery,credits,reels}/
│   ├── files/yash-khanna-resume.pdf
│   └── favicon.png
```

---

## Page-by-Page Content

| Page | Above the fold | Below |
|---|---|---|
| **Home** (`/`) | Hero image + name + tagline + showreel embed | Short intro paragraph, "View credits" CTA, recent gallery strip |
| **About** (`/about/`) | Strongest portrait + name | Bio (1–2 paragraphs), stats panel, "Download resume" button |
| **Credits** (`/credits/`) | "Filmography" headline | Credits grouped by category: Film → TV → Web → Theatre → Commercial → Voice → Short. Sorted by weight then year desc. |
| **Gallery** (`/gallery/`) | Headline | Responsive image grid (lightbox on click). Headshots and production stills, ordered by weight. |
| **Reels** (`/reels/`) | Headline | Showreel + scene clips + self-tapes, each with thumbnail + lazy embed. |
| **Contact** (`/contact/`) | "Get in touch" | Agent name/email/phone, Netlify Forms enquiry form, social/IMDb links |

Industry market = **India (Bollywood / OTT)**. English-only for v1 (industry standard for casting-facing material). Hugo multilingual can be retrofitted later if Hindi is wanted.

---

## Decap CMS Collections (`static/admin/config.yml`)

Mirror blueprint-builders' config (`/Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/blueprint-builders/static/admin/config.yml`): `backend: git-gateway`, `branch: main`, `media_folder` and `public_folder` per collection.

**Singletons (files collection "Pages"):**
- **home** — `name`, `tagline`, `hero_image` (image), `reel_url` (string, hint: YouTube or Vimeo URL), `intro` (text)
- **about** — `headline`, `bio` (markdown), object `stats` with: `height`, `eye_color`, `hair_color`, `age_range`, `playing_age`, `languages` (list), `accents` (list), `location`, `union_status` (select: Non-union / CINTAA / etc.), `representation` (string), `resume_pdf` (file widget)
- **contact** — `intro`, `email`, `agent_name`, `agent_email`, `agent_phone`, `socials` (list of `{label, url}` for IMDb, Instagram, Filmibeat, etc.)

**Folder collections:**
- **credits** — `content/credits/`. Fields: `title`, `category` (select: Film / Television / Web Series / Theatre / Commercial / Voice / Short), `role`, `production_company`, `director`, `year` (number), `still_image` (image, optional), `notes` (text, optional), `weight` (number — hint: lower number shows higher in list).
- **gallery** — `content/gallery/`. One file per image. Fields: `image` (required), `caption` (optional), `type` (select: Headshot / Production Still / Editorial), `weight`. *One-file-per-image is intentional* — keeps Decap's reorder model clear for parents and avoids list-widget array editing.
- **reels** — `content/reels/`. Fields: `title`, `url`, `thumbnail` (optional), `kind` (select: Showreel / Scene / Self-tape), `weight`.

Each collection gets `editor: { preview: false }` to keep the Decap UI simple for parents.

---

## Netlify Config

Copy `/Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/blueprint-builders/netlify.toml` and adapt:
- Swap domain in www→apex redirect block (use the registered domain Sumeet will provide).
- Keep security headers (X-Frame-Options, X-XSS-Protection, X-Content-Type-Options, Referrer-Policy).
- Keep 1-year cache headers for CSS/JS/PNG/JPG/WebP; add equivalent block for `*.pdf`.
- Pin `HUGO_VERSION` (0.146.0 or newer).
- Add Netlify Forms by putting `data-netlify="true" name="contact"` on the contact form `<form>` tag — no extra config required.

**Identity / Git Gateway one-time setup (Netlify dashboard, after first deploy):**
1. Site → Identity → Enable
2. Registration → **Invite only**
3. Services → Git Gateway → Enable
4. Invite a *throwaway test address* first to dry-run the flow before inviting parents.

---

## Critical Files to Reference / Copy

- `/Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/blueprint-builders/hugo.toml` — base config template
- `/Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/blueprint-builders/netlify.toml` — security headers, redirects, cache rules
- `/Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/blueprint-builders/static/admin/config.yml` — Decap collections shape & Git Gateway backend
- `/Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/blueprint-builders/static/admin/index.html` — copy verbatim
- `/Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/blueprint-builders/layouts/partials/` — reference for partial conventions (we'll write our own; PaperMod itself isn't reused)

## Verification

**Local Hugo dev:**
```bash
cd /Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/yash-khanna && hugo server -D
```
Walk every page at 360px / 768px / 1280px. Verify: hero image is LCP, reel iframe lazy-loads, resume PDF link returns 200, contact form posts without JS errors.

**Local Decap dev (before deploy):**
Add `local_backend: true` to `config.yml` (dev only). Then:
```bash
npx decap-server      # in one terminal
hugo server           # in another
```
Visit `http://localhost:1313/admin/`. Edit one credit, save, confirm a markdown file appears in `content/credits/` and the dev server rebuilds.

**Production handoff dry-run (critical):**
1. Deploy to Netlify, attach domain.
2. Enable Identity + Git Gateway; invite a throwaway address; complete the invite flow yourself.
3. From that account in Decap: (a) add a credit, (b) replace the resume PDF, (c) reorder a gallery image via `weight`, (d) change the reel URL. Confirm each appears live (~1 min build time).
4. Only after this works, invite parents and hand off a 1-page Loom video + printable cheat sheet ("How to add a credit", "How to swap a headshot", "How to update the reel").

**Pre-handoff checks:**
- Lighthouse > 95 on home (mobile)
- No console errors
- Contact form submission lands at target inbox
- Apex ↔ www redirect working both ways
- Resume PDF download works on mobile Safari

---

## Open Items (Sumeet to confirm before / during build)

1. **Domain** — registered, but exact value needed for `baseURL` in `hugo.toml` and the redirect block in `netlify.toml`. Sumeet to share.
2. **Yash's representation status** — has an agent to list, or "Seeking representation"? Affects whether the agent block is conditional.
3. **Analytics** — none for v1 (privacy-friendly, low overhead). Can add Plausible or GoatCounter later if wanted.
4. **Typography** — propose Google Fonts pairing of Cormorant Garamond (display) + Inter (body). Sumeet to approve or swap.
5. **Parent handoff format** — printed PDF cheat sheet, Loom video, or both.

initial-scale=1.0\">\n  <title>{{ block \"title\" . }}{{ if .IsHome }}{{ .Site.Title }} — Actor{{ else }}{{ .Title }} | {{ .Site.Title }}{{ end }}{{ end }}</title>\n  <meta name=\"description\" content=\"{{ block \"description\" . }}{{ with .Params.description }}{{ . }}{{ else }}{{ .Site.Params.description }}{{ end }}{{ end }}\">\n\n  <link rel=\"icon\" href=\"{{ .Site.Params.favicon | default \"/favicon.png\" }}\">\n\n  {{/* Open Graph */}}\n  <meta property=\"og:title\" content=\"{{ block \"title\" . }}{{ .Site.Title }}{{ end }}\">\n  <meta property=\"og:description\" content=\"{{ .Site.Params.description }}\">\n  <meta property=\"og:type\" content=\"website\">\n  <meta property=\"og:url\" content=\"{{ .Permalink }}\">\n  {{ with site.GetPage \"/\" }}{{ with .Params.hero_image }}<meta property=\"og:image\" content=\"{{ . | absURL }}\">{{ end }}{{ end }}\n\n  {{/* Fonts: Cormorant Garamond (display) + Inter (body) */}}\n  <link rel=\"preconnect\" href=\"https://fonts.googleapis.com\">\n  <link rel=\"preconnect\" href=\"https://fonts.gstatic.com\" crossorigin>\n  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Cormorant+Garamond:wght@400;500;600&family=Inter:wght@300;400;500;600&display=swap\">\n\n  {{/* Compiled stylesheet via Hugo Pipes */}}\n  {{ $style := resources.Get \"css/main.scss\" | resources.ToCSS (dict \"targetPath\" \"css/main.css\" \"outputStyle\" \"compressed\") | resources.Fingerprint }}\n  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"{{ $style.RelPermalink }}\" integrity=\"{{ $style.Data.Integrity }}\">\n</head>\n<body class=\"page-{{ .Kind }}{{ with .Section }} section-{{ . }}{{ end }}{{ if .IsHome }} is-home{{ end }}\">\n\n  {{ partial \"header.html\" . }}\n\n  <main id=\"main\">\n    {{ block \"main\" . }}{{ end }}\n  </main>\n\n  {{ partial \"footer.html\" . }}\n\n</body>\n</html>\n"
}

'true' : 'false');\n      btn.classList.toggle('is-open', open);\n    });\n  })();\n</script>\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/yash-khanna/layouts/partials/reel-embed.html",
  "content": "{{/*\n  Renders a lazy iframe embed for a YouTube or Vimeo URL.\n  Pass the URL as the context (e.g. {{ partial \"reel-embed.html\" .Params.reel_url }}).\n*/}}\n{{ $url := . }}\n{{ if $url }}\n  {{ $id := \"\" }}\n  {{ $provider := \"\" }}\n\n  {{/* YouTube */}}\n  {{ if or (strings.Contains $url \"youtube.com\") (strings.Contains $url \"youtu.be\") }}\n    {{ $provider = \"youtube\" }}\n    {{ if strings.Contains $url \"youtu.be/\" }}\n      {{ $id = index (split (index (split $url \"youtu.be/\") 1) \"?\") 0 }}\n    {{ else if strings.Contains $url \"v=\" }}\n      {{ $id = index (split (index (split $url \"v=\") 1) \"&\") 0 }}\n    {{ else if strings.Contains $url \"/embed/\" }}\n      {{ $id = index (split (index (split $url \"/embed/\") 1) \"?\") 0 }}\n    {{ end }}\n  {{ end }}\n\n  {{/* Vimeo */}}\n  {{ if strings.Contains $url \"vimeo.com\" }}\n    {{ $provider = \"vimeo\" }}\n    {{ $tail := index (split $url \"vimeo.com/\") 1 }}\n    {{ $id = index (split $tail \"?\") 0 }}\n    {{ $id = index (split $id \"/\") 0 }}\n  {{ end }}\n\n  {{ if and $id $provider }}\n    <div class=\"reel-embed\">\n      {{ if eq $provider \"youtube\" }}\n        <iframe src=\"https://www.youtube.com/embed/{{ $id }}?rel=0\" title=\"Showreel\" loading=\"lazy\" allow=\"accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture\" allowfullscreen></iframe>\n      {{ else if eq $provider \"vimeo\" }}\n        <iframe src=\"https://player.vimeo.com/video/{{ $id }}\" title=\"Showreel\" loading=\"lazy\" allow=\"autoplay; fullscreen; picture-in-picture\" allowfullscreen></iframe>\n      {{ end }}\n    </div>\n  {{ else }}\n    <p class=\"reel-embed reel-embed--fallback\"><a href=\"{{ $url }}\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\">Watch showreel ↗</a></p>\n  {{ end }}\n{{ end }}\n"
}
```

class=\"hero__eyebrow\">Actor</p>\n      <h1 class=\"hero__name\">{{ .Params.name | default .Site.Title }}</h1>\n      {{ with .Params.tagline }}<p class=\"hero__tagline\">{{ . }}</p>{{ end }}\n      <div class=\"hero__actions\">\n        <a class=\"btn btn--primary\" href=\"{{ \"/credits/\" | relURL }}\">View Credits</a>\n        {{ if $reel }}<a class=\"btn btn--ghost\" href=\"#reel\">Watch Showreel</a>{{ end }}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  </section>\n\n  {{ if $reel }}\n    <section id=\"reel\" class=\"home-reel\">\n      <div class=\"container\">\n        <h2 class=\"section-title\">Showreel</h2>\n        {{ partial \"reel-embed.html\" $reel }}\n      </div>\n    </section>\n  {{ end }}\n\n  {{ with .Params.intro }}\n    <section class=\"home-intro\">\n      <div class=\"container container--narrow\">\n        <p class=\"home-intro__text\">{{ . | markdownify }}</p>\n        <p><a class=\"link-arrow\" href=\"{{ \"/about/\" | relURL }}\">More about Yash →</a></p>\n      </div>\n    </section>\n  {{ end }}\n\n  {{/* Gallery strip — up to 6 headshots/stills by weight */}}\n  {{ $gallery := where (where .Site.RegularPages \"Section\" \"gallery\") \"Params.image\" \"!=\" nil }}\n  {{ if $gallery }}\n    {{ $gallery = sort $gallery \"Params.weight\" \"asc\" }}\n    <section class=\"home-gallery\">\n      <div class=\"container\">\n        <h2 class=\"section-title\">Gallery</h2>\n        <ul class=\"home-gallery__grid\">\n          {{ range first 6 $gallery }}\n            <li>\n              <a href=\"{{ \"/gallery/\" | relURL }}\">\n                <img src=\"{{ .Params.image | relURL }}\" alt=\"{{ .Params.caption | default \"Yash Khanna\" }}\" loading=\"lazy\">\n              </a>\n            </li>\n          {{ end }}\n        </ul>\n        <p><a class=\"link-arrow\" href=\"{{ \"/gallery/\" | relURL }}\">See all photos →</a></p>\n      </div>\n    </section>\n  {{ end }}\n\n  {{/* Latest credits — up to 4 by weight then     <li class=\"credit-list__item\">\n              <span class=\"credit-list__year\">{{ .Params.year }}</span>\n              <span class=\"credit-list__title\">{{ .Params.title }}</span>\n              <span class=\"credit-list__role\">{{ .Params.role }}</span>\n              <span class=\"credit-list__meta\">{{ .Params.category }}</span>\n            </li>\n          {{ end }}\n        </ul>\n        <p><a class=\"link-arrow\" href=\"{{ \"/credits/\" | relURL }}\">Full filmography →</a></p>\n      </div>\n    </section>\n  {{ end }}\n{{ end }}\n"
}

class=\"page__title\">{{ .Title }}</h1>\n        {{ with .Params.intro }}<p class=\"page__lede\">{{ . }}</p>{{ end }}\n      </header>\n\n      <div class=\"contact-grid\">\n\n        <section class=\"contact-block\">\n          <h2>Direct</h2>\n          {{ with .Params.email }}<p><a href=\"mailto:{{ . }}\">{{ . }}</a></p>{{ end }}\n        </section>\n\n        {{ if or .Params.agent_name .Params.agent_email .Params.agent_phone }}\n          <section class=\"contact-block\">\n            <h2>Representation</h2>\n            {{ with .Params.agent_name }}<p>{{ . }}</p>{{ end }}\n            {{ with .Params.agent_email }}<p><a href=\"mailto:{{ . }}\">{{ . }}</a></p>{{ end }}\n            {{ with .Params.agent_phone }}<p><a href=\"tel:{{ . }}\">{{ . }}</a></p>{{ end }}\n          </section>\n        {{ end }}\n\n        {{ with .Params.socials }}\n          <section class=\"contact-block\">\n            <h2>Elsewhere</h2>\n            <ul class=\"contact-socials\">\n              {{ range . }}\n                <li><a href=\"{{ .url }}\" rel=\"noopener\" target=\"_blank\">{{ .label }} ↗</a></li>\n              {{ end }}\n            </ul>\n          </section>\n        {{ end }}\n      </div>\n\n      <section class=\"contact-form-block\">\n        <h2>Casting Enquiries</h2>\n        <form name=\"contact\" method=\"POST\" data-netlify=\"true\" netlify-honeypot=\"bot-field\" action=\"/contact/?sent=1\" class=\"contact-form\">\n          <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"form-name\" value=\"contact\">\n          <p class=\"hp\"><label>Don't fill this out if you're human: <input name=\"bot-field\"></label></p>\n\n          <div class=\"form-row\">\n            <label for=\"name\">Name</label>\n            <input id=\"name\" name=\"name\" type=\"text\" required>\n          </div>\n\n          <div class=\"form-row\">\n            <label for=\"email\">Email</label>\n            <input id=\"email\" name=\"email\" type=\"email\" required>\n          </div>\n\n          <div class=\"form-row\">\n            <label for=\"project\">Project / Production</label>\n            <input id=\"project\" name=\"project\" type=\"text\">\n          </div>\n\n          <div class=\"form-row\">\n            <label for=\"message\">Message</label>\n            <textarea id=\"message\" name=\"message\" rows=\"5\" required></textarea>\n          </div>\n\n          <button class=\"btn btn--primary\" type=\"submit\">Send Enquiry</button>\n        </form>\n      </section>\n    </div>\n  </article>\n{{ end }}\n"

  "content": "// ============================================================\n// Yash Khanna — Actor Portfolio\n// Custom stylesheet. Mobile-first.\n// ============================================================\n\n// ---- Tokens ------------------------------------------------\n\n$display: 'Cormorant Garamond', Georgia, serif;\n$body: 'Inter', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', sans-serif;\n\n$ink: #111;\n$ink-soft: #444;\n$ink-mute: #777;\n$paper: #fbfaf7;     // warm off-white\n$paper-deep: #f3f0e9;\n$line: #e3dfd5;\n$accent: #8a6a3b;    // muted brass\n\n$radius: 2px;\n$ease: cubic-bezier(.2,.7,.2,1);\n\n$break-md: 720px;\n$break-lg: 1024px;\n\n// ---- Reset / Base ------------------------------------------\n\n*, *::before, *::after { box-sizing: border-box; }\n\nhtml {\n  font-size: 16px;\n  scroll-behavior: smooth;\n  -webkit-text-size-adjust: 100%;\n}\n\nbody {\n  margin: 0;\n  font-family: $body;\n  font-weight: 400;\n  line-height: 1.6;\n  color: $ink;\n  background: $paper;\n  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;\n  text-rendering: optimizeLegibility;\n}\n\nimg, video, iframe { max-width: 100%; display: block; }\nimg { height: auto; }\n\na {\n  color: $ink;\n  text-decoration: underline;\n  text-decoration-thickness: 1px;\n  text-underline-offset: 3px;\n  transition: color .2s $ease;\n}\na:hover { color: $accent; }\n\nh1, h2, h3, h4 {\n  font-family: $display;\n  font-weight: 500;\n  letter-spacing: -0.01em;\n  line-height: 1.1;\n  margin: 0 0 .6em;\n}\n\nh1 { font-size: clamp(2.25rem, 5vw, 3.5rem); }\nh2 { font-size: clamp(1.5rem, 3vw, 2rem); }\nh3 { font-size: 1.25rem; }\n\np { margin: 0 0 1em; }\n\n// ---- Layout primitives ------------------------------------\n\n.container {\n  width: 100%;\n  max-width: 1180px;\n  margin: 0 auto;\n  padding-left: 1.25rem;\n  padding-right: 1.25rem;\n}\n.container--narrow { max-width: 760px; }\n\n.section-title {\n  font-size: clamp(1.25rem, 2vw, 1.6rem);\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: .2em;\n  font-family: $body;\n  font-weight: 500;\n  color: $ink-soft;\n  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;\n  &::after {\n    content: '';\n    display: block;\n    width: 40px;\n    height: 1px;\n    background: $accent;\n    margin-top: .75rem;\n  }\n}\n\n.link-arrow {\n  font-family: $body;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: .15em;\n  font-size: .8rem;\n  text-decoration: none;\n  border-bottom: 1px solid currentColor;\n  padding-bottom: 2px;\n}\n\n// ---- Buttons -----------------------------------------------\n\n.btn {\n  display: inline-block;\n  padding: .85em 1.6em;\n  font-family: $body;\n  font-size: .85rem;\n  font-weight: 500;\n  letter-spacing: .15em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  text-decoration: none;\n  border: 1px solid $ink;\n  background: $ink;\n  color: $paper;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  transition: all .2s $ease;\n  border-radius: $radius;\n}\n.btn:hover { background: $accent; border-color: $accent; color: $paper; }\n\n.btn--ghost {\n  background: transparent;\n  color: $paper;\n  border-color: $paper;\n}\n.btn--ghost:hover { background: $paper; color: $ink; border-color: $paper; }\n\n// ---- Header -----------------------------------------------\n\n.site-header {\n  position: sticky;\n  top: 0;\n  z-index: 50;\n  background: rgba(251, 250, 247, .92);\n  backdrop-filter: blur(8px);\n  border-bottom: 1px solid $line;\n}\n.site-header--home {\n  position: absolute;\n  inset: 0 0 auto 0;\n  background: transparent;\n  backdrop-filter: none;\n  border-bottom: none;\n  .site-header__name { color: $paper; }\n  .primary-nav a { color: $paper; }\n  .nav-toggle__bar { background: $paper; }\n}\n\n.site-header__inner {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  padding-top: 1rem;\n  padding-bottom: 1rem;\n}\n\n.site-header__brand { text-decoration: none; }\n.site-header__name {\n  font-family: $display;\n  font-size: 1.4rem;\n  font-weight: 500;\n  letter-spacing: .04em;\n}\n\n.primary-nav ul {\n  list-style: none;\n  display: flex;\n  gap: 2rem;\n  margin: 0;\n  padding: 0;\n}\n.primary-nav a {\n  text-decoration: none;\n  font-size: .8rem;\n  letter-spacing: .2em;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  font-weight: 500;\n}\n.primary-nav a[aria-current=\"page\"] { color: $accent; }\n\n.nav-toggle {\n  display: none;\n  background: transparent;\n  border: 0;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  padding: .5rem;\n  width: 44px;\n  height: 44px;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  justify-content: center;\n  gap: 5px;\n}\n.nav-toggle__bar {\n  display: block;\n  width: 22px;\n  height: 1.5px;\n  background: $ink;\n  transition: transform .2s $ease, opacity .2s $ease;\n}\n.nav-toggle.is-open .nav-toggle__bar:nth-child(1) { transform: translateY(7px) rotate(45deg); }\n.nav-toggle.is-open .nav-toggle__bar:nth-child(2) { opacity: 0; }\n.nav-toggle.is-open .nav-toggle__bar:nth-child(3) { transform: translateY(-7px) rotate(-45deg); }\n\n@media (max-width: #{$break-md - 1px}) {\n  .nav-toggle { display: flex; }\n  .primary-nav {\n    position: absolute;\n    top: 100%; left: 0; right: 0;\n    background: $paper;\n    border-bottom: 1px solid $line;\n    display: none;\n  }\n  .primary-nav.is-open { display: block; }\n  .primary-nav ul {\n    flex-direction: column;\n    gap: 0;\n    padding: 1rem 1.25rem;\n  }\n  .primary-nav li { border-bottom: 1px solid $line; }\n  .primary-nav li:last-child { border-bottom: 0; }\n  .primary-nav a { display: block; padding: .9rem 0; }\n  .site-header--home .primary-nav { background: rgba(20,20,20,.95); }\n}\n\n// ---- Hero (home) -------------------------------------------\n\n.hero {\n  position: relative;\n  min-height: 90vh;\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  color: $paper;\n  background-color: #111;\n  background-image: var(--hero-image, none);\n  background-size: cover;\n  background-position: center 20%;\n  padding: 8rem 0 4rem;\n}\n.hero__overlay {\n  position: absolute;\n  inset: 0;\n  background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(0,0,0,.4) 0%, rgba(0,0,0,.55) 100%);\n}\n.hero__inner {\n  position: relative;\n  z-index: 1;\n}\n.hero__eyebrow {\n  font-family: $body;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: .35em;\n  font-size: .75rem;\n  opacity: .85;\n  margin-bottom: 1rem;\n}\n.hero__name {\n  font-size: clamp(2.8rem, 8vw, 5.5rem);\n  line-height: .95;\n  margin: 0 0 1rem;\n  color: $paper;\n}\n.hero__tagline {\n  font-family: $display;\n  font-style: italic;\n  font-size: clamp(1.1rem, 2vw, 1.4rem);\n  max-width: 38ch;\n  opacity: .9;\n  margin-bottom: 2rem;\n}\n.hero__actions {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\n  gap: 1rem;\n}\n\n// ---- Home sections -----------------------------------------\n\n.home-reel,\n.home-intro,\n.home-gallery,\n.home-credits {\n  padding: 4rem 0;\n  border-bottom: 1px solid $line;\n}\n.home-intro__text {\n  font-family: $display;\n  font-size: clamp(1.2rem, 2.2vw, 1.6rem);\n  line-height: 1.5;\n  color: $ink-soft;\n}\n\n.home-gallery__grid {\n  list-style: none;\n  margin: 0; padding: 0;\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);\n  gap: .75rem;\n  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;\n}\n@media (min-width: $break-md) {\n  .home-gallery__grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr); }\n}\n@media (min-width: $break-lg) {\n  .home-gallery__grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(6, 1fr); }\n}\n.home-gallery__grid img {\n  width: 100%;\n  aspect-ratio: 3 / 4;\n  object-fit: cover;\n  filter: grayscale(.1);\n  transition: filter .3s $ease, transform .3s $ease;\n}\n.home-gallery__grid a:hover img { filter: none; transform: scale(1.02); }\n\n// ---- Reel embed --------------------------------------------\n\n.reel-embed {\n  position: relative;\n  aspect-ratio: 16 / 9;\n  background: #000;\n  border-radius: $radius;\n  overflow: hidden;\n  margin: 0 0 1.5rem;\n}\n.reel-embed iframe {\n  position: absolute;\n  inset: 0;\n  width: 100%;\n  height: 100%;\n  border: 0;\n}\n.reel-embed--fallback {\n  aspect-ratio: auto;\n  background: transparent;\n  padding: 2rem;\n  text-align: center;\n}\n\n// ---- Pages -------------------------------------------------\n\n.page { padding: 5rem 0; }\n.page__header { margin-bottom: 2.5rem; }\n.page__title {\n  font-size: clamp(2rem, 5vw, 3.25rem);\n  margin-bottom: .5rem;\n}\n.page__lede {\n  font-family: $display;\n  font-style: italic;\n  font-size: 1.25rem;\n  color: $ink-soft;\n}\n\n.page__body {\n  font-size: 1.05rem;\n  line-height: 1.75;\n}\n.page__body p { margin-bottom: 1.25em; }\n\n.page__cta { margin-top: 2.5rem; }\n\n// ---- About -------------------------------------------------\n\n.about__portrait {\n  margin: 0 0 2.5rem;\n  img {\n    width: 100%;\n    max-height: 560px;\n    object-fit: cover;\n    border-radius: $radius;\n  }\n}\n\n.stats-panel {\n  margin: 2.5rem 0 1rem;\n  border-top: 1px solid $line;\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: 1fr;\n}\n.stats-panel__row {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: 1fr 2fr;\n  gap: 1rem;\n  padding: .85rem 0;\n  border-bottom: 1px solid $line;\n  font-size: .95rem;\n}\n.stats-panel dt {\n  font-family: $body;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: .12em;\n  font-size: .75rem;\n  color: $ink-mute;\n  font-weight: 500;\n}\n.stats-panel dd {\n  margin: 0;\n  color: $ink;\n}\n\n// ---- Credits -----------------------------------------------\n\n.credits-group {\n  margin: 0 0 3rem;\n}\n.credits-group__title {\n  font-size: clamp(1.5rem, 2.5vw, 1.9rem);\n  margin-bottom: 1rem;\n  padding-bottom: .5rem;\n  border-bottom: 1px solid $line;\n}\n\n.credits-table {\n  list-style: none;\n  padding: 0; margin: 0;\n}\n.credits-row {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: 70px 1fr;\n  gap: .5rem 1rem;\n  padding: 1rem 0;\n  border-bottom: 1px solid $line;\n  align-items: baseline;\n}\n@media (min-width: $break-md) {\n  .credits-row {\n    grid-template-columns: 70px 2fr 2fr 2fr;\n    gap: 1.25rem;\n  }\n}\n.credits-row__year {\n  font-family: $display;\n  font-size: 1.05rem;\n  color: $ink-mute;\n}\n.credits-row__title {\n  font-weight: 500;\n}\n.credits-row__role,\n.credits-row__meta {\n  color: $ink-soft;\n  font-size: .95rem;\n}\n\n.credit-list { list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 0; }\n.credit-list__item {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: 60px 1fr;\n  gap: .5rem 1rem;\n  padding: .85rem 0;\n  border-bottom: 1px solid $line;\n  align-items: baseline;\n}\n@media (min-width: $break-md) {\n  .credit-list__item { grid-template-columns: 60px 2fr 2fr 1.2fr; }\n}\n.credit-list__year { font-family: $display; color: $ink-mute; }\n.credit-list__role, .credit-list__meta { color: $ink-soft; font-size: .9rem; }\n\n// ---- Gallery -----------------------------------------------\n\n.gallery-grid {\n  list-style: none;\n  padding: 0; margin: 0;\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);\n  gap: 1rem;\n}\n@media (min-width: $break-md) {\n  .gallery-grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr); gap: 1.25rem; }\n}\n@media (min-width: $break-lg) {\n  .gallery-grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr); }\n}\n.gallery-grid__item img {\n  width: 100%;\n  aspect-ratio: 3 / 4;\n  object-fit: cover;\n  border-radius: $radius;\n  transition: transform .3s $ease;\n}\n.gallery-grid__item a:hover img { transform: scale(1.02); }\n.gallery-grid__caption {\n  margin: .5rem 0 0;\n  font-size: .85rem;\n  color: $ink-mute;\n}\n\n// ---- Reels list --------------------------------------------\n\n.reels-list {\n  display: grid;\n  gap: 3rem;\n}\n.reel-card__title {\n  font-size: 1.5rem;\n  margin-bottom: 1rem;\n}\n.reel-card__kind {\n  display: inline-block;\n  font-family: $body;\n  font-size: .7rem;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: .15em;\n  color: $accent;\n  margin-left: .5rem;\n  vertical-align: middle;\n}\n\n// ---- Contact -----------------------------------------------\n\n.contact-grid {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: 1fr;\n  gap: 2rem;\n  margin-bottom: 3rem;\n}\n@media (min-width: $break-md) {\n  .contact-grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr); }\n}\n.contact-block h2 {\n  font-family: $body;\n  font-size: .8rem;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: .2em;\n  color: $ink-mute;\n  margin-bottom: .75rem;\n}\n.contact-block p { margin: 0 0 .25em; }\n\n.contact-socials {\n  list-style: none;\n  padding: 0; margin: 0;\n}\n.contact-socials li { margin-bottom: .35em; }\n\n.contact-form-block {\n  border-top: 1px solid $line;\n  padding-top: 2.5rem;\n}\n.contact-form-block h2 {\n  font-family: $display;\n  font-size: 1.6rem;\n  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;\n}\n\n.contact-form .form-row {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  margin-bottom: 1.25rem;\n}\n.contact-form label {\n  font-size: .8rem;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: .15em;\n  color: $ink-mute;\n  margin-bottom: .4rem;\n}\n.contact-form input,\n.contact-form textarea {\n  font-family: $body;\n  font-size: 1rem;\n  padding: .75rem;\n  border: 1px solid $line;\n  background: $paper;\n  border-radius: $radius;\n  color: $ink;\n  resize: vertical;\n}\n.contact-form input:focus,\n.contact-form textarea:focus {\n  outline: 2px solid $accent;\n  outline-offset: 1px;\n  border-color: $accent;\n}\n.contact-form .hp { position: absolute; left: -9999px; }\n\n// ---- Footer ------------------------------------------------\n\n.site-footer {\n  background: $paper-deep;\n  margin-top: 4rem;\n  padding: 3rem 0 1.5rem;\n  border-top: 1px solid $line;\n}\n.site-footer__inner {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: 1fr;\n  gap: 1.5rem;\n}\n@media (min-width: $break-md) {\n  .site-footer__inner { grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr 1fr; align-items: start; }\n}\n.site-footer__name {\n  font-family: $display;\n  font-size: 1.4rem;\n  margin: 0;\n}\n.site-footer__role {\n  font-size: .8rem;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: .2em;\n  color: $ink-mute;\n  margin: 0;\n}\n.site-footer__contact a { display: inline-block; margin-bottom: .25em; }\n.site-footer__rep {\n  font-size: .9rem;\n  color: $ink-soft;\n  margin-top: .5rem;\n}\n.site-footer__socials {\n  list-style: none;\n  padding: 0; margin: 0;\n  display: flex;\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\n  gap: 1rem;\n}\n.site-footer__socials a {\n  font-size: .8rem;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: .15em;\n}\n.site-footer__credits {\n  border-top: 1px solid $line;\n  margin-top: 2rem;\n  padding-top: 1rem;\n  font-size: .8rem;\n  color: $ink-mute;\n}\n.site-footer__credits p { margin: 0; }\n"
}

  "new_string": "  {{ $pageTitle := cond .IsHome (printf \"%s — Actor\" .Site.Title) (printf \"%s | %s\" .Title .Site.Title) }}\n  <title>{{ $pageTitle }}</title>\n  <meta name=\"description\" content=\"{{ with .Params.description }}{{ . }}{{ else }}{{ .Site.Params.description }}{{ end }}\">\n\n  <link rel=\"icon\" href=\"{{ .Site.Params.favicon | default \"/favicon.png\" }}\">\n\n  {{/* Open Graph */}}\n  <meta property=\"og:title\" content=\"{{ $pageTitle }}\">\n  <meta property=\"og:description\" content=\"{{ .Site.Params.description }}\">\n  <meta property=\"og:type\" content=\"website\">\n  <meta property=\"og:url\" content=\"{{ .Permalink }}\">\n  {{ with site.GetPage \"/\" }}{{ with .Params.hero_image }}<meta property=\"og:image\" content=\"{{ . | absURL }}\">{{ end }}{{ end }}"
}

**What's at `/Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/yash-khanna/`:**
- Hugo site with custom layouts (no theme) — full-bleed hero, restrained serif+sans typography, mobile-first responsive
- 6 pages: Home, About, Credits (grouped Film/TV/Web/Theatre/Commercial/Voice/Short), Gallery, Reels, Contact
- Decap CMS at `/admin/` with parent-friendly field hints; collections: Pages (Home/About/Contact singletons), Credits, Gallery (one-file-per-image), Reels
- Netlify Forms wired into contact form
- `netlify.toml` mirrors blueprint-builders security/cache/redirect patterns
- Placeholder content seeded across all sections so the site looks complete on first deploy

**To preview locally:** `cd /Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/yash-khanna && hugo server -D` → http://localhost:1313/

**Before you deploy** (open items from the plan):
1. Replace `yashkhanna.com` in `hugo.toml` + `netlify.toml` with the actual registered domain
2. Drop the real hero image, headshots, and resume PDF into `static/img/` and `static/files/`
3. After first Netlify deploy: enable Identity + Git Gateway, invite a throwaway test address first, then parents
4. The placeholder reel URLs are Rickroll/Vimeo demos — swap before going live

> DEVELOPER

Its asking me to sign into the decap but what should I use?

> AGENT

Decap doesn't work out of the box — it needs auth wired up. Two paths depending on where you're testing:

## If you're testing **locally** (`localhost:1313/admin/`)

Use Decap's local proxy — no Netlify needed.

1. Add `local_backend: true` to the top of `static/admin/config.yml`:

> TOOL

id: toolu_01A3s2tDkKs8DSffK2Axa6qj

{

  "old_string": "backend:\n  name: git-gateway\n  branch: main",
  "new_string": "local_backend: true\n\nbackend:\n  name: git-gateway\n  branch: main"
}
```

> TOOL

tool_result
id: toolu_01A3s2tDkKs8DSffK2Axa6qj
```
The file /Users/sumeet/matrix/reliq/yash-khanna/static/admin/config.yml has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)
```

> AGENT

2. In one terminal: `npx decap-server` (proxy that writes directly to your filesystem, no login)
3. In another: `hugo server` (already running)
4. Visit `http://localhost:1313/admin/` — it'll skip auth and connect to the local proxy. Edits write straight to `content/`.

**Important:** `local_backend: true` is for dev only. Remove it (or leave it — it's ignored unless decap-server is running) before going live. It's safe to leave in.

## If you've **deployed to Netlify** and you see the Identity login

You need to enable Identity on the Netlify side (one-time):

1. Netlify dashboard → your site → **Identity** → **Enable Identity**
2. **Registration preferences** → set to **Invite only** (so randoms can't sign up)
3. **Services → Git Gateway** → **Enable Git Gateway** (this is what lets Decap actually commit to your repo)
4. **Identity → Invite users** → invite your own email first (do a test run before inviting Yash's parents)
5. Check your inbox → click the invite link → set a password → it'll redirect back to `/admin/` logged in

The login button you're seeing right now is the Netlify Identity widget — until you've done steps 1–4, there's nothing to log into.

**Which one is it — local or deployed?** If you tell me, I can be more specific.

It'll prompt to install `decap-server` the first time — say yes. Once it prints `Decap CMS Proxy Server listening on port 8081`, refresh `http://localhost:1313/admin/`.

The login screen should disappear and you'll land straight in the CMS. Any edits write directly to files under `content/` — you'll see them update in your editor in real time.

If it still asks you to log in after the proxy is running, hard-refresh (Cmd+Shift+R) — the browser may have cached the auth widget.

That fixes the 404s on About + Gallery. Two things still 404 that need your attention:

1. **Home hero image** — the URL you have in `content/_index.md` right now is `https://www.zee5.com/kids/kids-shows/chhota-bheem/0-6-1894`, which is a **webpage**, not an image file. Browsers can't display it. The Hero Image field needs either:

   - a URL ending in `.jpg`/`.png`/`.webp` (e.g. paste a Picsum URL: `https://picsum.photos/seed/yash-hero/1600/900`)

2. **Resume PDF** — the About page links to `/files/yash-khanna-resume.pdf`, which doesn't exist yet. Either upload one through Decap (About → Stats Panel → Resume PDF), or clear that field for now so the "Download Resume" button doesn't render.

> DEVELOPER

notice that in some of the D cap CMS, some of the entries just have the title, for example in the gallery, there's one entry in the CMS that's just for gallery. I don't want to have that, I don't want that to be editable and I don't want them to get fused confused. So in every CMS entity it should only be adding new information, not changing things like headers, for example. Does that make sense? Just confirm with me before you start changing.

> AGENT

Makes total sense. Let me confirm what I'm seeing and what I'd change before touching anything.

**What's causing the phantom "Gallery" entry:**
The folder `content/gallery/` contains an `_index.md` file (the page-header file, holds just `title: "Gallery"`). Decap's folder collections show *every* `.md` file in the folder, so `_index.md` shows up as a row alongside real photos. Same issue exists for Credits (`_index.md` holds `title: "Filmography"`) and Reels (`title: "Reels"`).

**Other "header-like" things that are currently editable but shouldn't be:**
- The About page's title field (`Page Title: About`) — parents could accidentally rename "About" to something else.
- The Contact page's title field (`Page Title: Contact`) — same risk.
- Home doesn't have a header-rename risk — every field on Home is actual content (Name, Tagline, etc.).

**My proposed fix (waiting for your OK):**

1. Delete the three `_index.md` files in `content/credits/`, `content/gallery/`, `content/reels/`. Hugo doesn't need them — it generates the section pages automatically. The layouts already fall back to a default title ("Filmography", "Gallery", "Reels") I hardcoded.
2. Remove the "Page Title" field from the About and Contact singletons in `static/admin/config.yml`, and hardcode those titles into the layouts. Parents will edit body / bio / contact details but the page name stays locked.

Net effect for parents in Decap: the only things they see are real content rows (a photo, a credit, a reel) and the editable fields inside Home/About/Contact — no phantom "Gallery"-type entries, no editable page headers.

Sound good? Or do you want me to keep the `_index.md` files visible somewhere else (e.g. for editing the optional intro text on a section page)?

Accepts a dict with optional keys:\n    - file:    path to a locally uploaded video (preferred if set)\n    - url:     YouTube or Vimeo URL\n    - poster:  optional thumbnail/poster image\n  Backwards-compatible: a bare string is treated as a URL.\n*/}}\n{{ $file := \"\" }}\n{{ $url := \"\" }}\n{{ $poster := \"\" }}\n{{ if reflect.IsMap . }}\n  {{ $file = .file }}\n  {{ $url = .url }}\n  {{ $poster = .poster }}\n{{ else }}\n  {{ $url = . }}\n{{ end }}\n\n{{ if $file }}\n  <div class=\"reel-embed reel-embed--local\">\n    <video controls preload=\"metadata\"{{ with $poster }} poster=\"{{ . | relURL }}\"{{ end }}>\n      <source src=\"{{ $file | relURL }}\">\n      Your browser doesn't support embedded video. <a href=\"{{ $file | relURL }}\">Download the video</a>.\n    </video>\n  </div>\n{{ else if $url }}\n  {{ $id := \"\" }}\n  {{ $provider := \"\" }}\n\n  {{/* YouTube */}}\n  {{ if or (strings.Contains $url \"youtube.com\") (strings.Contains $url \"youtu.be\") }}\n    {{ $provider = \"youtube\" }}\n    {{ if strings.Contains $url \"youtu.be/\" }}\n      {{ $id = index (split (index (split $url \"youtu.be/\") 1) \"?\") 0 }}\n    {{ else if strings.Contains $url \"v=\" }}\n      {{ $id = index (split (index (split $url \"v=\") 1) \"&\") 0 }}\n    {{ else if strings.Contains $url \"/embed/\" }}\n      {{ $id = index (split (index (split $url \"/embed/\") 1) \"?\") 0 }}\n    {{ end }}\n  {{ end }}\n\n  {{/* Vimeo */}}\n  {{ if strings.Contains $url \"vimeo.com\" }}\n    {{ $provider = \"vimeo\" }}\n    {{ $tail := index (split $url \"vimeo.com/\") 1 }}\n    {{ $id = index (split $tail \"?\") 0 }}\n    {{ $id = index (split $id \"/\") 0 }}\n  {{ end }}\n\n  {{ if and $id $provider }}\n    <div class=\"reel-embed\">\n      {{ if eq $provider \"youtube\" }}\n        <iframe src=\"https://www.youtube.com/embed/{{ $id }}?rel=0\" title=\"Showreel\" loading=\"lazy\" allow=\"accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture\" allowfullscreen></iframe>\n      {{ else if eq $provider \"vimeo\" }}\n        <iframe src=\"https://player.vimeo.com/video/{{ $id }}\" title=\"Showreel\" loading=\"lazy\" allow=\"autoplay; fullscreen; picture-in-picture\" allowfullscreen></iframe>\n      {{ end }}\n    </div>\n  {{ else }}\n    <p class=\"reel-embed reel-embed--fallback\"><a href=\"{{ $url }}\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\">Watch showreel ↗</a></p>\n  {{ end }}\n{{ end }}\n"

Repo is up at https://github.com/kungfusaini/yash-khanna and `main` is pushed.

**Next step — your turn (one minute):**

```bash
netlify login
```

That opens a browser window for OAuth. Once you're logged in, tell me and I'll create the Netlify site linked to the repo and trigger the first deploy.

While you do that, here's the DNS picture so you can have your registrar tab ready:

**For the website (Netlify):**
After I create the site, Netlify will give you an apex IP (currently `75.2.60.5`) and an alias (`<sitename>.netlify.app`). At your registrar for `yashkhanna.in`:
- `A` record at `@` → `75.2.60.5`
- `CNAME` at `www` → `<sitename>.netlify.app`

**For email (Mailcow):**
Your Mailcow instance has its own hostname (e.g. `mail.yourdomain.tld`). At your `yashkhanna.in` registrar:
- `MX` at `@` → `mail.yourdomain.tld` (priority 10)
- `TXT` at `@` for SPF → `v=spf1 mx ~all`
- `TXT` at `dkim._domainkey` (or whatever selector Mailcow shows you) → the DKIM key Mailcow generates after you add the domain
- `TXT` at `_dmarc` → `v=DMARC1; p=quarantine; rua=mailto:postmaster@yashkhanna.in`

The actual values come from your Mailcow admin → *Mail Setup* → *Domains* → add `yashkhanna.in`. It'll print you the exact SPF, DKIM, and MX records.

Ping me when `netlify login` finishes and I'll handle the site creation + deploy.

> AGENT

**Where we are:** site is live at https://yashkhanna-in.netlify.app — all 5 pages return 200. Custom domain `yashkhanna.in` is registered with Netlify (cert will provision once DNS resolves).

**Three things now need you in a browser. Do them in this order:**

### 1. Hook GitHub up to Netlify (5 min)
Without this, Decap saves from parents won't trigger rebuilds.

- Go to https://app.netlify.com/projects/yashkhanna-in/configuration/deploys
- Click **"Link repository"** → choose **GitHub** → authorize → select `kungfusaini/yash-khanna`
- Build settings should auto-populate (`hugo --gc --minify`, publish `public`)

### 2. Configure DNS at your registrar for yashkhanna.in

| Type | Host | Value | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `A` | `@` | `75.2.60.5` | Netlify's apex load balancer |
| `CNAME` | `www` | `yashkhanna-in.netlify.app` | |

After propagation (a few minutes to a few hours), Netlify auto-provisions the Let's Encrypt cert. Visit `https://app.netlify.com/projects/yashkhanna-in/configuration/domain` once DNS is live to verify.

### 3. Enable Identity + Git Gateway (so /admin works in production)
At https://app.netlify.com/projects/yashkhanna-in:

- **Site configuration** → **Identity** → **Enable Identity**
- **Registration preferences** → set to **Invite only**
- Scroll to **Services** → **Git Gateway** → **Enable Git Gateway**
- Then **Identity** → **Invite users** → invite your own email first as a dry-run, then Yash's parents

Once you've done #1 and #3, remove the `local_backend: true` line from `static/admin/config.yml` and push — production Decap will then authenticate against Netlify Identity instead of trying the local proxy.

**Email/Mailcow** — leave it as-is until you've got mail flowing. When you're ready, share your Mailcow domain hostname and I'll give you the exact MX/SPF/DKIM/DMARC values for the registrar.

I'll walk you through all three. We'll do everything in Netlify first (one browser tab), then registrar last (another tab). Tell me when each step is done and I'll verify.

---

## Step 1 — Enable Identity + Git Gateway

This makes the `/admin/` page actually log in.

1. Open: https://app.netlify.com/projects/yashkhanna-in
2. Left sidebar → **Site configuration** → **Identity**
3. Click **Enable Identity**
4. In the same page, scroll to **Registration preferences** → click **Edit settings** → set to **Invite only** → Save. (Stops random people from signing up.)
5. Scroll further down to **Services** → find **Git Gateway** → click **Enable Git Gateway**. (Netlify may prompt to authenticate with GitHub — this is the same OAuth that step 2 uses, so doing it now kills two birds.)

→ **Tell me when done.** I'll verify Identity is enabled before you move on.

---

## Step 2 — Link the GitHub repo for auto-deploys

So Decap saves trigger production rebuilds.

1. Same Netlify project, sidebar → **Site configuration** → **Build & deploy** → **Continuous deployment**
2. Find the **Repository** section. It'll show a yellow warning that the link isn't fully set up (because of the host-key error we saw).
3. Click **Link repository** (or **Configure repo** / **Manage repository** — wording varies).
4. Choose **GitHub** → authorize Netlify if prompted → on the GitHub side, choose **Only select repositories** → pick `kungfusaini/yash-khanna` → **Install**.
5. Back on Netlify, build settings should already say `hugo --gc --minify` and publish dir `public` (from when I set them via API). If it asks, confirm those.
6. Click **Save** or **Deploy site** — Netlify will now try a fresh build from the GitHub source, which should succeed this time.

→ **Tell me when done.** I'll trigger a tiny verification commit so we both see auto-deploy fire.

---

## Step 3 — DNS at your registrar

Switch to your registrar's DNS panel for `yashkhanna.in`. (Tell me which registrar if you want UI-specific instructions — GoDaddy / Namecheap / BigRock / Cloudflare etc. all have the same data but different click paths.)

Add these two records:

| Type | Host / Name | Value | TTL |
|---|---|---|---|
| `A` | `@` (or leave blank — means root) | `75.2.60.5` | default (1h is fine) |
| `CNAME` | `www` | `yashkhanna-in.netlify.app` | default |

**Delete any old A/CNAME records on `@` and `www`** if they exist, otherwise they fight.

Propagation: usually 5–30 min, sometimes a few hours. Once DNS resolves:
- Netlify auto-provisions a free Let's Encrypt SSL cert (takes 1–5 min after DNS is live)
- `https://yashkhanna.in` starts serving the site
- `www.yashkhanna.in` 301-redirects to apex (your `netlify.toml` already handles this)

→ **Tell me when DNS is saved.** I'll check propagation with `dig` and confirm the cert provisions.

I wanna point the squarespace to claoudlfar and do it from them

> AGENT

Great call — Cloudflare DNS is faster to propagate, has a way better UI, and you'll need it later when we add Mailcow records anyway. Here's the full sequence:

---

## A. Add yashkhanna.in to Cloudflare

1. Go to https://dash.cloudflare.com → sign up or log in (free tier is fine)
2. Click **Add a site** → enter `yashkhanna.in` → **Continue**
3. Pick the **Free** plan → **Continue**
4. Cloudflare will scan Squarespace's DNS for existing records. Review what it finds and **delete any** that point to old Squarespace site infrastructure (you're moving DNS to point at Netlify now). Keep anything email-related if Squarespace ever issued you email — but if you've never used Squarespace for mail, there'll likely be nothing critical.
5. On the next screen, Cloudflare gives you **two nameservers** like:
   ```
   xxx.ns.cloudflare.com
   yyy.ns.cloudflare.com
   ```
   **Copy these — you'll paste them into Squarespace next.**

---

## B. Switch nameservers at Squarespace

1. Go to https://account.squarespace.com/domains
2. Click `yashkhanna.in`
3. Look for **DNS settings** or **Nameservers** (Squarespace recently moved this under **Domain settings → Advanced settings → Nameservers**)
4. Select **Use custom nameservers**
5. Delete Squarespace's default nameservers, paste the two from Cloudflare
6. **Save**

→ Squarespace will warn that this will disable Squarespace-managed DNS. That's exactly what you want — Cloudflare takes over. Confirm.

---

## C. Wait for Cloudflare to detect the change

Back in Cloudflare → it'll be polling for the nameserver change. Usually 5–30 minutes, sometimes faster. You'll get an email "Cloudflare is now protecting yashkhanna.in" when it's done. You can also click **"Check nameservers"** to nudge it.

---

## D. Add the Netlify DNS records in Cloudflare

Once Cloudflare is "active" for the domain, go to **DNS → Records → Add record**:

| Type | Name | Target / IPv4 address | Proxy status | TTL |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `A` | `@` | `75.2.60.5` | **DNS only (gray cloud)** | Auto |
| `CNAME` | `www` | `yashkhanna-in.netlify.app` | **DNS only (gray cloud)** | Auto |

### ⚠️ Critical: gray cloud, NOT orange cloud

Netlify provides its own CDN and SSL. If you leave Cloudflare's proxy on (orange cloud), you get a redirect loop and the Netlify-issued SSL cert can't provision. **Click the orange cloud icon on each record to toggle it gray.** Save.

(You can revisit the orange-cloud route later with `Full (strict)` SSL mode, but DNS-only is the right starting point and works perfectly.)

---

## E. Wait for propagation, then Netlify auto-provisions SSL

- DNS propagation through Cloudflare is fast — usually 1–5 min.
- Once `dig yashkhanna.in` returns `75.2.60.5`, Netlify detects it and provisions a Let's Encrypt cert (1–5 min more).
- Then https://yashkhanna.in starts serving the site.

**When you've finished steps A–D, tell me and I'll run `dig` to verify propagation and watch the cert come up.**

1. **Now**: Step A/B above (Cloudflare nameserver swap — starts the propagation clock)
2. **While Cloudflare is detecting**: do Netlify **Identity + Git Gateway** (my last message's "Step 1")
3. **While Identity is enabling**: do the **GitHub App link** (my last message's "Step 2")
4. **When Cloudflare goes active**: come back, add the A + CNAME (my Step D above)

Three productive things in parallel.

## To enable the API path:

1. Go to https://dash.cloudflare.com/profile/api-tokens → **Create Token** → **Get started** (under "Custom token")
2. Name it something like `yash-khanna-setup`
3. **Permissions** — add these three:
   - **Account** | **Account Settings** | **Read**
   - **Zone** | **Zone** | **Edit**
   - **Zone** | **DNS** | **Edit**
4. **Account Resources** → **Include** → your Cloudflare account
5. **Zone Resources** → **Include** → **All zones from an account** → your account
6. Create → **copy the token**, you won't see it again

Then paste it back here like:
```
CF_API_TOKEN=<paste>
```

I'll add the zone via API, read back the nameservers, and tell you the exact two strings to paste into Squarespace. Then while propagation runs, you handle Netlify Identity + GitHub App, and when CF goes active I add the records.

**Worth it?** Honestly, for a one-domain setup, doing the whole thing in the Cloudflare UI is ~6 clicks total (sign up, add site, copy NS, then later add 2 records). The CLI/API route adds the overhead of token creation. Up to you — say "yes API" or "I'll just click through the UI" and I'll proceed accordingly.

## Now do Step 1 — Enable Identity + Git Gateway

1. Open: https://app.netlify.com/projects/yashkhanna-in
2. Top nav → **Site configuration** (the gear-ish icon, left sidebar)
3. Scroll left sidebar → click **Identity**
4. Click the big **Enable Identity** button
5. Once enabled, click **Registration preferences** → **Edit settings** → set to **Invite only** → **Save**
6. Scroll down to **Services** section → find **Git Gateway** → click **Enable Git Gateway**
   - It may say "Sign in with GitHub" — click that, authorize. This sets up the OAuth link to your GitHub account so Netlify can commit on behalf of logged-in Identity users.

→ **Ping me when done** and I'll do a quick API check that Identity is on.

---

## Then Step 2 — Install Netlify GitHub App (for auto-deploys)

1. Same Netlify project page
2. **Site configuration → Build & deploy → Continuous deployment** (in left sidebar)
3. You'll see a warning that the repo isn't accessible. Click **Link repository** (or **Configure** if shown)
4. Choose **GitHub** as the provider
5. On the GitHub side: **Only select repositories** → tick `kungfusaini/yash-khanna` → **Install & Authorize**
6. Back on Netlify: confirm the build settings already say:
   - Branch: `main`
   - Build command: `hugo --gc --minify`
   - Publish directory: `public`
7. Click **Save**

Netlify will trigger a build immediately to verify the link works. That build should succeed within ~30 seconds (it's a tiny site).

→ **Ping me when done.** I'll push a no-op commit and we'll watch the auto-deploy fire to prove the loop works end-to-end.

- They log into `https://yashkhanna.in/admin/` with their Identity invite
- Their edits commit to GitHub via Git Gateway
- Netlify auto-builds and deploys
- Site updates within ~1 minute

Here's where we are:

✅ **Identity** — confirmed on
✅ **Git Gateway** — confirmed on (the `/.netlify/git/settings` endpoint returns the expected 401-needs-token, which only happens when it's enabled)
✅ **Form detection** — I flipped it on
✅ **SSL on `yashkhanna.in`** — live with valid cert
❌ **GitHub auto-deploy** — **not actually working.** I pushed a test commit (`6e3ac97`) ~6 minutes ago and Netlify never picked it up. So the Netlify GitHub App isn't installed on `kungfusaini/yash-khanna` despite the linking attempt.

## To verify on your side:

Open https://github.com/settings/installations — look for **Netlify** in the list.

- If it's not there → the install never happened
- If it's there → click **Configure** → check the repository list → if it shows "All repositories" or includes `kungfusaini/yash-khanna`, the app has access. If `yash-khanna` is missing, add it.

## Quickest fix path:

Visit https://github.com/apps/netlify/installations/new → pick **Only select repositories** → tick `yash-khanna` → **Install**.

Once that's done, push something and it'll trigger a build within ~10 seconds. I can push a no-op commit again to test the moment you tell me it's installed.

(Or if you'd rather skip continuous deploys for now — fine, but Decap saves from parents won't auto-publish, you'd have to deploy manually each time.)

> AGENT

Confirmed the problem: even when I trigger a build directly, Netlify can't clone the repo — same `Host key verification failed` error. So the GitHub App is installed at the GitHub account level (you confirmed), but Netlify hasn't been told *"use that installation for this specific site."* That linkage is what makes the deploy key get added.

This step isn't doable via API — it's done through the Netlify dashboard's repo-linking flow which establishes the deploy credentials.

## Do this:

1. Go to https://app.netlify.com/projects/yashkhanna-in/configuration/deploys
2. Scroll to **"Continuous deployment"** section
3. You'll see something like:
   - **Repository**: shows `kungfusaini/yash-khanna` (because I set it via API)
   - Probably a yellow warning that says something like *"Repo not accessible"* or *"Reconfigure repository"*
4. Click whatever button is offered: **"Link to a different repository"** or **"Manage repository"** or **"Reconfigure"**
5. Choose **GitHub** → it'll see your existing Netlify App installation and just ask you to **confirm/authorize** linking this specific repo to this site → confirm
6. Build settings should already be `hugo --gc --minify` / publish `public` — confirm those if asked
7. Save

This second pass adds the deploy key so Netlify can actually `git clone` from the repo.

When done, tell me and I'll trigger another test build — should succeed and finally prove the loop works end to end.

> AGENT

Confirmed the root cause via GitHub API: **zero deploy keys on the repo** (`/repos/.../keys` returned `[]`). Netlify says the repo is "linked" but the clone credentials were never added — that's why every build fails at "Host key verification failed".

The fix is via the **"Manage repository"** button in that exact panel.

## Do this:

1. Click **"Manage repository"**
2. You'll likely get options like:
   - **Unlink** / **Disconnect from Git provider**
   - or **Reconnect with GitHub App**
   - or **Configure deploy credentials**
3. Best path: **Unlink / Disconnect**, then immediately re-link by clicking the **"Link repository"** or **"Connect to a Git repository"** button that appears
4. When you re-link, choose **GitHub** → it should detect the App is already installed on `kungfusaini/yash-khanna` → just confirm
5. Build settings should auto-fill — confirm `hugo --gc --minify` and `public`

That re-link will issue the deploy key (or set up the App-based HTTPS clone, depending on which method Netlify defaults to now).

After that, I'll trigger a test build again — should clone successfully this time.

Tell me what options appear when you click "Manage repository" — if the path I described doesn't show, I'll adjust.

> AGENT

Got it. Two notes from reading the file:

1. Your Mailcow hostname is actually **`mail.sumeetsaini.com`**, not `mail.reliq.digital` — no problem, we'll use the real one.
2. Mailcow's suggested SPF is `v=spf1 -all` which means *"no server is authorized to send for this domain"* — that'd break mum's outgoing replies. I'm overriding to `v=spf1 mx -all` so the MX (mail.sumeetsaini.com) is authorized.

Here are the **8 DNS records** to add at Cloudflare → DNS → Records → Add record:

---

| # | Type | Name | Content | Priority/Port | Proxy |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `MX` | `@` | `mail.sumeetsaini.com` | Priority `10` | DNS only |
| 2 | `CNAME` | `autodiscover` | `mail.sumeetsaini.com` | — | **DNS only (gray)** |
| 3 | `CNAME` | `autoconfig` | `mail.sumeetsaini.com` | — | **DNS only (gray)** |
| 4 | `SRV` | `_autodiscover._tcp` | (see below) | — | n/a |
| 5 | `TXT` | `@` | `v=spf1 mx -all` | — | n/a |
| 6 | `TXT` | `_dmarc` | `v=DMARC1; p=reject; sp=reject; adkim=s; aspf=s` | — | n/a |
| 7 | `TXT` | `dkim._domainkey` | (see below — DKIM key) | — | n/a |

**For the SRV record (#4)**, Cloudflare's form will ask for fields separately:
- Service: `_autodiscover`
- Protocol: `_tcp`
- Name: `@` (or yashkhanna.in)
- Priority: `0`
- Weight: `0`
- Port: `443`
- Target: `mail.sumeetsaini.com`

**For the DKIM TXT (#7)** — paste this as the content (one long string, no line breaks):

```
v=DKIM1;k=rsa;t=s;s=email;p=MIIBIjANBgkqhkiG9w0BAQEFAAOCAQ8AMIIBCgKCAQEAtaKV6z+IBwQpQ7RMpnm3FKHA7wuYGq9yDWHUD0o4ELHfCPK22HdjAHYiXjx2HJ3M7Y+mDK41P5oG/fpn6WbcExnUGEvp9yhmlfgfcNfERg/BZeXH6FLeLG+E4nuSzAIDcKVAhCC5cE/2FOhyylHGnrhanWGg30oCDxkXjiI1dTvgrCgpmdm3Gccn3dO13CNlemRmdJs/wDYzzavVcMpfhUd/aEZZqNoY8JLddpslEwkRnhzoqk9q0/ieJyW4iOIiurP/i0vOfR5X4Iwo1PV6ynDu7YR+Zz5fInpWoAj/K4ketPV85xrYsdWazxvkKemfBYaTDyS/jbwSTLcIl9WOowIDAQAB
```

**I skipped the `@ TXT ?` line** from the file — that's a Mailcow placeholder, nothing to add.

3. Set the contact mailbox to also forward (so even mail sent specifically to `contact@` reaches mum's inbox):

   - Scroll to **ACL / Sender ACL / forwarding** area → look for **Forward all messages to** → enter `<MUM_EMAIL>`

   - **Save**

> AGENT

**⚠️ Big problem found:** Mailcow's TLS certificate for `mail.sumeetsaini.com` **expired Jan 29 2026** (4 months ago). SMTP/IMAP still answer TCP, but:
- Gmail's "Send mail as" verification will fail
- Modern clients (including Apple Mail) refuse to connect via STARTTLS with an expired cert
- Strict-policy servers sending TO this domain may reject delivery

Mailcow auto-renews via Let's Encrypt; something's been broken in the renewal loop. Need to fix before mum's mail setup will work end-to-end.

**Quick diagnosis (SSH to the Mailcow host):**
```bash
cd /opt/mailcow-dockerized
docker compose logs acme-mailcow --tail 200 | grep -iE "error|fail|cert"
```

Common causes:
- Port 80 isn't reachable for HTTP-01 challenge (firewall, reverse proxy)
- `additional_san=` in `mailcow.conf` has a hostname that no longer resolves
- The container's been stopped

**Force-renew once it's diagnosed:**
```bash
docker compose exec acme-mailcow /srv/acme/acme.sh --renew-all --force
# or in newer Mailcow:
docker compose exec acme-mailcow acme.sh --force-renew -d mail.sumeetsaini.com
# or just:
docker compose restart acme-mailcow
```

---

## On the forwarding question

In recent Mailcow versions there isn't a "Forward all" field directly on the mailbox edit page anymore. Three paths that work:

### Easiest: SOGo Sieve filter (5 clicks)
1. Open https://mail.sumeetsaini.com in a browser → log in as `contact@yashkhanna.in` with the mailbox password
2. Top right → **Preferences** (gear icon)
3. Left tab → **Mail** → **Filters**
4. **+ Create filter**, call it "Forward to mum":
   - Condition: leave the default ("Match any of"), then change to **"Match all of"** with no rules — i.e. matches every message
   - (If SOGo requires at least one rule: pick **"Subject contains"** → leave value empty — matches everything)
   - Action 1: **Keep** (so a copy stays in contact@'s inbox)
   - Action 2: **Redirect to** → `<MUM_EMAIL>`
5. **Save** and make sure the filter is **Active**

### Alternative: BCC via Mailcow admin
1. Email → Configuration → Mailboxes → pencil-edit `contact@`
2. Scroll for a "**BCC map**" section (or it might be elsewhere — search the page for "BCC")
3. If you find it: add destination = mum's email, type = Recipient, direction = Inbound
4. Save

### Alternative: convert to alias (loses send-as)
Delete the mailbox, replace with a `contact@yashkhanna.in` alias → mum. Simpler but mum can no longer reply *as* contact@ via SMTP.

---

**My recommendation:** fix the TLS cert first. Until that's renewed, send-as setup will fail and we're chasing a dead end. Once cert is fresh, do the Sieve filter and we'll do an end-to-end test (I'll send a test email to a few addresses at yashkhanna.in and confirm they land in mum's inbox).
